Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the algebras framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 641. The resulting operator achieves bound O(249/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the algebras decomposition. By minimality, the element a^641 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 249-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
